Therefore, those who have been laid off after March 15, 2020 have been granted unemployment allowance and $1,177 in cash support for those who are unable to benefit from short work grants. Our business owner, who has requested short work grants due to a negative impact on the Coronavirus, is able to apply via e-mail to the ISKUR unit where he is connected. Our tradesman, who temporarily reduces his weekly working hours by at least one-third or stops his work at work for at least four weeks, without looking for a continuity requirement, must apply to İSKUR in his municipality of residence to benefit from short work. Turkey to the chairman of the Grand National Assembly The New Koronavirus (Covid-19) New Coronavirus, which is currently in session, proposes and proposes the change of Article 12 of the Law Proposal for the Resolving of the Influence of Economics and Social Life (Covid-19) in the following way. With all due respect. The Turkish Trade Law No. 6102, dated 12-13/01/2011, added the following temporary clause. A decision can be made to distribute the net-term profits of 2019 until 30/9/2020 in capital companies, not to distribute profits and free backups in the past year, cannot be given to the board of directors in general. More than 50 per cent of the state's private administration, the municipality, the village and other public corporate people and capital do not apply this ban on companies with which public funds directly or indirectly have more than 50 per cent of their capital. The President is in charge of extending and shortening the period specified in this joke for three months. (2) Payments on over 25 per cent of net-term profits in 2019 are postponed until the end of the period mentioned in the first joke, if the decision to distribute profits for the account period has not yet been made or a partial payment has been paid to the shareholders." (3) Capital companies with debt restructuring agreements with financial institutions and committed to paying more than 25 per cent of net-term profits (1). It's an exception to a joke.
